# PilotODIN | Engineering decision index and cross-cutting decisions

This is an index of decisions, not a parallel requirements document. The Master Reconstruction Blueprint and explicit owner decisions remain normative. The relevant specification owns technical contracts; the phase policy owns permissions; the live operational state owns next work (CONT-D02; the legacy handoff is now a compatibility route).

## CONT-D01 | Interruption-resilient continuation

- **Date / status:** 24 November 2026; adopted by owner request to implement the proposed continuity improvements.
- **Problem:** planned handoff was strong, but important in-flight work might exist only in an interrupted chat. The prior documentation audit demonstrated status drift caused by repeated receipts.
- **Decision:** one replace-in-place work journal, pre-operation intent, stable task and operation IDs, Git evidence reconciliation, and single canonical receipt. The protocol requires a checkpoint before consequential actions and a post-operation checkpoint. Permanent decision rationale stays in its owning specification or this index for cross-cutting process decisions.
- **Alternatives considered:** transcript-dependent recovery (fragile and context-heavy); duplicate full histories in all live documents (drift); new database/service or automatic native operation (unnecessary scope and dependencies). Retain the existing Git-backed documentation and add minimal structured state.
- **Limits:** no guaranteed recovery of private reasoning, unsaved local edits, inaccessible tools, or external actions that leave no verifiable receipt. Expected-HEAD checks are optimistic concurrency controls, not exclusive locks. Ambiguous restricted dispatch is treated as potentially consumed, not permission to retry.
- **Verification:** [recovery checks](./CONTINUATION_RECOVERY_CHECKS.md); [journal](./ACTIVE_WORK_JOURNAL.md); [protocol](./CONTINUATION_PROTOCOL_CURRENT.md). No product or acceptance change.

## Decision recording rule

For a material design, safety, scope or evidence-method decision, record an ID, date, status (proposed/accepted/superseded/blocked), question, selected option, rejected alternatives, rationale, affected invariants, limitations, evidence, approval requirement and reopen condition. Write it before a consequential action where feasible; link from the journal. If an existing specification already owns it, update that specification and add a link here only if cross-cutting navigation needs it. Preserve superseded decisions and point to the replacement. A proposed method or agent recommendation never substitutes for owner acceptance or new authority.

## 27 November | Owner-approved C5 applicability policy and consolidated Stage A transaction

**Decision C5-APPLICABILITY-20261127 (adopted; preparation only).** Owner explicitly stated: "I approve the simplified C5 development policy as proposed. Resolve the remaining concrete blockers and prepare the consolidated Stage A execution transaction without weakening product requirements or inferring restricted execution authority." This resolves the C5 method proposal in the migration audit §3 only. Finite, applicability-based qualification of standard development tools within C-D09: known publisher, exact observed identity/path/version/ABI, applicable security and internal-use rights, compatibility, fixed bounded operations and checkable functional output. No recursive independent qualification of provider-managed verification tools within the approved trust boundary. Preserve independent expected application archive hashes, use-time rehash, actual applicable notices/component rights and security, exact locks, tests, all product/data/privacy invariants, original scope and existing fail-closed safeguards. No use of an unresolved applicable vulnerability. Do not introduce a new runtime or verifier merely to qualify another one. Directly acquired assets are not provider-managed by inference. This does not authorise acquisition, installation, native execution, migration, paid resources, production cutover, altered acceptance criteria, D/G2 evidence changes or phase advance. No standing consolidated execution grant. Prior audit and decisions remain historical at their scopes.

**Specific qualification findings (public publisher metadata/source review, no executable activity):**

- GitHub CLI 2.105.0: [2.106.0 release](https://github.com/cli/cli/releases/tag/v2.106.0) enumerates seven fixes. GHSA-5v78-vjjq-j2fj, GHSA-3j6c-526c-w465, GHSA-q2rq-2r2v-2jm5, GHSA-mjfw-qpmw-c8p9 and GHSA-mrhx-xw2w-4289 involve Codespaces, agent-task or skills commands excluded here. [GHSA-45h6-2fh4-pr93](https://github.com/cli/cli/security/advisories/GHSA-45h6-2fh4-pr93) terminal controls and [GHSA-4946-22jg-pg7m](https://github.com/cli/cli/security/advisories/GHSA-4946-22jg-pg7m) verbose/debug HTTP output require controls: no interactive terminal, pager, raw console or debug logs; remove GH_DEBUG and other debug/force-TTY settings, no verbose flag, use fixed args/absolute path, capture both streams as bounded untrusted data, publish only validated fixed fields. Never interpret raw output as commands or success. Publisher recommends upgrade; prefer a supported patched managed CLI if already available and specifically reviewed. Existing 2.105.0 is a conditional candidate only, not security clearance; actual identity, control enforcement, applicable dependency/security and rights disposition remain before use. Primary MIT terms reviewed at v2.105.0 LICENSE b6a58a9572cbd7e4550f8205a9c4fd199cd442f2. Full advisory retrieval was unavailable for the C1 issue; source/release scope is evidence, not a blanket clean bill.
- Exact CLI source: [release verify](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/release/verify/verify.go) c1a9ae4a20a019133f1bac6ef4d5039eda00e5cc emits structured verified results with format JSON; [attestation verify](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/attestation/verify/verify.go) 4f04c4d21cc256ecf2093112d42e6f1ed9f90dab supports bundle/custom trusted root and exact identity policy. [API client](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/attestation/api/client.go) 7ad342d03bb9253c5c56dd4e26526250daf27c6c has retries, pagination and possible external bundle URLs; a CLI process timeout or --limit alone is not proof of bounded network. [Sigstore implementation](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/attestation/verification/sigstore.go) 60c347ef88d17eb6ac3761ebffe55515c7321723 uses TUF initialization unless custom root supplied. [Documented offline option](https://docs.github.com/en/actions/how-tos/secure-your-work/use-artifact-attestations/verify-attestations-offline) is a supported candidate only with authenticated bundle and trusted root, not a provenance waiver. No custom cryptographic verifier, new tool download, recursive host proof or open-ended GH control traffic. Actual bounded control metadata route (endpoints, redirects/retries, response/request caps, trusted-root custody, credentials) remains a hard pre-execution dependency.
- Managed Windows tools: reviewed runner image win22/20261123.376 (20261123.376.1) advertises cached CPython 3.14.10 and Visual Studio Enterprise 17.14.37906.3. [Microsoft release history](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/visualstudio/releases/2022/release-history) maps the latter to 17.14.45, 10 November 2026; [servicing policy](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/visualstudio/releases/2022/servicing-vs2022) identifies supported 17.14. Scope DUMPBIN to /HEADERS and /IMPORTS of only the verified necessary PE member(s); no DLL load, debugger, IDE, MSBuild, GNU/ELF tools or broad binary dump. [Documented imports](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/cpp/build/reference/imports-dumpbin?view=msvc-170) include normal/delay imports, not all static/dynamic composition. Publisher build/notice evidence still needed. Exact on-runner executable identity, applicable rights/security and actual observations remain conditional. CP314 inspection only, not CP311 test witness. No blanket qualification from version inventory.
- Settlement: existing [inspection_adapter.py](../qa/c5_managed_dev/inspection_adapter.py) 39f485c5241884443cd7dfdf76a60040f25b11f9 is Linux-only for process capture and contains POSIX-only member staging assumptions. Existing [product Job Object implementation](../third_party/tauri-plugin-shell-2.6.0/src/process/job_object.rs) a9c1a3bcdc52a4d75710b33c034d9cd4b3bdd45d already demonstrates the source pattern: own unnamed job, assign suspended process, resume, retain handle, query active process count, terminate if required. [Windows Job Objects](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/procthread/job-objects) and [assignment](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/jobapi2/nf-jobapi2-assignprocesstojobobject) support nested jobs with conditions. Reuse this narrow ownership pattern, not a new general collector, a foreign PID kill, an assumed empty job, a bare subprocess timeout or reliance on runner teardown. Existing C5 Windows adapter is not implemented; actual assign-before-resume and positive zero-process settlement must be shown before cleanup/success. No new product native compilation or run.

One conditional transaction, with explicit pre-use hold point: (1) verify exact source, tools and scope; authenticate Astral release attestation and selected asset digest and build provenance using standard verification, exact repository/signer/subject/source facts, not presumed equality of workflow and release commit; bounded acquisition only after exact fresh grant; (2) independently verify the exact nine archives before parsing, use existing inert inventory helper 2b4f2acec0d996b884bd107e8bfb533668d3750f and targeted actual notices/linkage/security/rights, including runtime bundled components and pip vendor terms; do not execute/extract unqualified bytes; if any disposition cannot be independently established during the approved transaction, stop before use with bounded facts, no automatic continuation or second acquisition; (3) only after confirmed pre-use clearance, stage verified runtime into owned scratch, check exact CP311 x64 identity, use existing venv/pip adapter with --isolated, --no-index, --only-binary=:all:, --require-hashes, no-cache and approved wheelhouse; compare dry-run/install reports and installed graph, no implicit resolution or network; (4) invoke existing validation_coordinator.py pytest_witness and receipt_pair only for the one expected file and node set; **do not call run_profile**, which unconditionally enters Stage B after A. Exact detached source activation diff and runtime 3.11.18 standalone binding required before any execution, no modification to original assertions, no environment-variable bypass or live disarm removal. Preserve one genuine negative guard test; do not claim 19 independent product features. (5) positively settle all owned processes and scratch before bounded redacted final receipt. Do not publish raw notices, binary bytes, private paths, credentials or uncontrolled output to the public repository. No B, Linux, fonts, Rust, frontend, installer or product migration under this transaction. All remaining C5 and D/P8 requirements retained.

**Readiness disposition: BLOCKED, not permission to run.** Actual image/path/tool suitability and rights, authenticated release and asset provenance, exact control-plane budget/enforcement, actual runtime/PDFium/pip/other wheel dispositions, Windows staging/settlement, and exact one-use standalone activation source remain missing. The existing TAR reader may refuse actual publisher format; refuse rather than relax archive safety by inference. No new synthetic suite, runner poll, verifier, workflow or execution. Next address only the concrete bounded-control/settlement adapter and pre-use qualification feasibility using existing standard tools; if no supported route meets retained boundaries, state formal no-go and seek distinct G2 method decision rather than another general preparation loop. A later source-pinned execution approval is required and must be withheld until the proposed exact transaction is ready. No general D1/D2 method decision or execution grant inferred. All four profiles blocked, C5 4/8, source 14/17, official P4 14/20, 45 consumed/disarmed, zero active; no P5, installer frozen. Unknown owner-local work, 28 detached records and original/historical data preserved.

**Publication and evidence:** current parent 15c479c2ffd4c5874b542def9f998fa7b21c41c3; exact HEAD/queued/in-progress run queries zero; previous audit diff contains only documentation/state, 120 workflows unchanged; reuse previous audited trigger scope for the same decision-index and state paths. No restricted action, actual acquisition, package import, native execution, test run or product/lock/workflow edit. This is the single decision/preparation record, not a further acceptance milestone.

## 27 November | C5 standard provenance route stopping decision

**C5-STAGE-A-PROVENANCE-STOP-20261127: current route NO-GO under retained bounds, not a general impossibility claim.** Parent 916665c10d1e3e4f2c87064d11a0afb0d98823ee. Owner's generic next continues approved applicability-based preparation only. No new method or execution authority. Targeted review of exact GitHub CLI v2.105.0 [release verify](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/release/verify/verify.go#L118-L176), [release verify-asset](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/release/verify-asset/verify_asset.go), [attestation API client](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/attestation/api/client.go#L139-L260) and [attestation verify](https://github.com/cli/cli/blob/v2.105.0/pkg/cmd/attestation/verify/verify.go#L160-L240). Both release commands fetch tag ref and attestations through live clients even with custom trusted root; hard-coded 100 attestation selection limit, pagination, three retry allowance per page, parallel external bundle retrieval and uncapped io.ReadAll followed by Snappy decode. The result-count limit is not a request/byte/redirect bound. Fixed commands, noninteractive output and a 30-minute timeout cannot establish exact network caps. Offline artifact build-attestation verification with local bundle/root is distinct and cannot by itself prove the required GitHub release attestation. No approved authenticated local release-evidence supply or enforcement mechanism identified. A custom TLS/HTTP mediation layer, additional verifier or OS/network policy change would reintroduce the preparatory loop and is not proposed or authorized. No claim all patched versions or future standard-tool routes are impossible.

**Stop further standalone A preparation under this route.** Keep exact plan bd7bf96a067af2277728818b6aa4f759f6babac9, candidate 0c1590f57c4d62d58dca482c03ac9948009e90a4, both locks, nine archive identities, 19 predicates and all prior evidence disarmed/preserved. Windows assign-before-resume/owned Job Object settlement remains a supported source pattern but actual runner implementation/settlement and pre-use facts remain unresolved; do not implement/test another adapter independently of a viable end-to-end route. No CLI use, attestation acquisition, archive download, installation, native run, synthetic test, product/lock/workflow change or C5 closure. A standard method with an exact authenticated release/build witness and enforceable bounds can reopen C5; any different evidence method or managed control-traffic risk boundary needs a distinct explicit decision, not inference.

**Next decision, independent G2 feasibility only:** follow the controlling [G2 consolidated decision](./P4_R02_5_G2_CONSOLIDATED_CLOSURE_DECISION_2026-11-24.md#decision-and-finite-options), unchanged blob 8906f34d7e3c29f16417506985e7a14da9afe319. Recommend explicit owner approval to assess/design an equivalent independent operation-level physical-file-object witness, replacing a literal original issuing user-mode HANDLE requirement only where equivalent evidence can be independently demonstrated. Retain complete original writer/parent/thread attribution, object epoch/request/completion, actual physical destination/volume/file identity, alias/reparse and rename/link source/destination, all mutation classes and background provenance, complete loss-reviewed same-capture coverage, zero unexplained mandatory gaps, and original data/privacy/network restrictions. Retain exact original process handle/ETW lifetime plus independent Sysmon corroboration without undocumented identical distinct timestamp fields. This is a proposal for bounded feasibility/design, not an approved revised method, D implementation, native approval, or P4 advance. Do not write a collector, load a driver, change OS settings, access owner data, run G2, or reopen speculative diagnostics. If owner does not agree, hold blocked. G2 cannot close until an independently feasible method and actual evidence exist; G3/G4, D1/D2 and physical 8 GB evidence remain required. No waiver.

**Source and evidence:** current n12_physical_destination.py babeaac8f207510162f848d15b696b6c1df4653d requires original file handle plus request epoch, admitted root handle and independent corroboration; its native supplied-handle observation returns UNVERIFIED_HANDLE_PROVENANCE, not authenticated writer. n12_fileio_pair_contract.py 83a3933ce9cac8f09db886e2ee030c720fbd449e indexes original handle/FileObject/IRP and writer epoch for writes only; returns candidate-only even on consistent inputs. No silent change to either, no foreign handle duplication or reopened-by-name substitute. D1 requirements ce8cbe509a91823cbb37bbb62f30dc953d3ad664 retain actual production identifier, bundled engine, frontend and profile path, generated data, actual lifecycle and separate network evidence; external dev journey not equivalent. Historical run 45342345634: 23 prohibited, 4,921 unresolved including 125 network-process path gaps, 102 lexical candidates only; 74 historic original-data differences with unknown writer remain separate. All historic failures retained.

**Selected conditional design:** a filesystem observer independent of application code, potentially a reviewed Windows Filter Manager minifilter, plus existing ETW/held-process-handle/Sysmon lifecycle evidence. No product driver or dependency proposed. Each observed operation would need unique capture/volume/instance/object-open epoch, request and completion identity (including non-IRP paths), original issuer process/thread epoch and parent hierarchy, normalized destination and same-object file/volume identity, requested operation and final status/effect, coverage and loss evidence. For rename/link/delete preserve pre/post identity, source/destination and actual outcome; for alias/reparse/case/stream uncertainty refuse classification. Physical admitted roots independently observed and protected identities/aliases preserved, not lexical prefix alone. No raw names, pointers, PIDs or private traces published. Use the existing strict reducer as historical baseline; a future separately approved equivalent reducer must preserve every outcome and negative case.

| Existing blocker | Design contribution / remaining proof |
| --- | --- |
| B01 | Preserve exact original-process handle/ETW birth/exit and independent Sysmon GUID/image/sequence/time-window corroboration; do not equate distinct timestamp semantics or introduce arbitrary tolerance. Actual all-process join not verified. |
| B02 | Complete independent start/exit/thread inventory including short-lived children, PID reuse and pre-existing processes; no polling-only completeness. Not demonstrated. |
| B03 | Bind WebView/engine/app/launcher hierarchy to original process epochs, parent sequence and image; all descendants and ambiguous roles must resolve. Not demonstrated. |
| B04 | Bind effective production WebView UDF, root identity and observed actual destination; no config/path-string substitution or dev override. Actual production profile evidence absent. |
| B05 | Candidate operation-level FileObject epoch instead of literal numeric issuing file HANDLE; documented Filter Manager callback surface supports observation, not proven equivalence. |
| B06 | Request/completion correlation using observer-owned unique IDs, object epoch and statuses, cross-check with independent ETW where applicable; include fast I/O, cached, paging and nonwrite classes, deduplication and missing completions. No complete join demonstrated. |
| B07 | Same-object query for file/volume ID, observed normalized destination and rename/link completion correction. File IDs can be reused; post-create/query-context limits, alias/reparse and actual volume identity unresolved. |
| B08 | All 125 WebView network-process file path gaps require actual witnessed destination, no lexical candidate credits or attribution to network activity by name. No historical gaps resolved. |
| B09 | All mutation classes and all scoped/other/unattributed operations included, including delayed cache and mapped-page writes. Original writer attribution for shared mapped writes is a hard feasibility gap. No historical gap resolved. |
| B10 | Capture before first startup through final owned settlement and delayed-write observation; independently review attached volumes/filter scope, operation classes, sequence gaps/drop/overflow counters, ETW coverage and same-capture positive/negative write/restore controls. No complete loss/coverage claim from observer self-report. |
| B11 | Signed/supported authorized disposable environment, verified scope, privacy-safe bounded local trace, reviewed disclosure and independently confirmed settlement/cleanup. No approved driver/tool or environment, no native authority. |

**Evidence supporting the design and its limit:** Microsoft [FltGetFileNameInformation](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltgetfilenameinformation) restricts name queries in paging I/O and other contexts; [FltGetDestinationFileNameInformation](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltgetdestinationfilenameinformation) and [FltGetTunneledName](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltgettunneledname) require completion-time correction for tunneled create/rename/link names. [FltQueryInformationFile](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltqueryinformationfile) queries the actual FileObject, [FILE_ID_INFORMATION](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/ns-ntifs-file_id_information) exposes volume/file identity, [FILE_INTERNAL_INFORMATION](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/ns-ntifs-_file_internal_information) warns IDs may be reused. [FltGetRequestorProcessId](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltgetrequestorprocessid) identifies the requestor thread's associated process, not guaranteed original memory writer. [File caching](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/fileio/file-caching) describes OS-delayed lazy writes; [section synchronization](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ifs/flt-parameters-for-irp-mj-acquire-for-section-synchronization) exposes section creation, not each CPU store into shared mapped memory. Inference: a later write-back record and mapping inventory cannot alone distinguish which of two processes modified a shared page. No invented attribution to System, nearest writer or section opener. Do not prohibit memory mapping in product or weaken original attribution to make evidence pass. [Filter Manager](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ifs/filter-manager-concepts), [driver INF](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ifs/creating-an-inf-file-for-a-minifilter-driver) and [test-signing guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/install/introduction-to-test-signing) demonstrate additional kernel deployment/signing obligations. No new signing infrastructure, security setting change or driver build proposed. ProcMon documented operation/process/stack capture remains corroboration only, not proof of every original writer, physical object identity and complete loss-reviewed coverage. Endpoint snapshots or VM isolation alone cannot prove transient operation compliance. No alternative proven.

**Finite feasibility gate and disposition:** before implementation, a supported independently reviewed observer must demonstrate a credible mechanism for original attribution including shared mapped/cache/background writes, complete operation/volume coverage, object/destination identity in restricted callback contexts, independent loss detection, and safe supported deployment without prohibited OS changes. No route currently satisfies all. Stop at G2 BLOCKED; no new collector, generated fixture, driver, or repeated research campaign. If those concrete facts later become available, separately agree the exact method and one finite calibration plus actual production-equivalent run, both separately authorised; G2 can pass with fully attributed violations, G3 fixes and G4 requires zero prohibited/unresolved mutations. D1/D2, R02.6, physical 8 GB, original data and P8 remain unchanged. No score increase.

**Practical next owner discussion (proposal only):** both current C5 provenance and G2 full-attribution routes are blocked. Prefer reopening C5 with a narrowly revised managed-development provenance control-traffic boundary for standard GitHub release/build attestation commands, rather than embarking on a kernel-driver project. Preserve exact cryptographic release/build provenance, archive sizes/hashes, rights/security, all functional predicates, no product egress change. Explicitly distinguish provider-managed control traffic (internal retries/pagination/bundle/TUF fetches; no claim of exact request/byte limit) from exact nine application/runtime archive acquisition limits. Any acceptance of this residual risk requires a distinct owner decision, exact supported tool/commands, one disposable job, time/output limits, no extra acquisition/OS change, and separately approved execution. Not an adopted exception or execution permission; if unacceptable hold both blocked and seek a viable alternative, not more infrastructure. No automatic phase advance.
